### [1] [PhantomSeal: Proactive Deepfakes Defense with Identity/Context Protection and Forensic Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20564)
*Liangqin Ren,Zeyan Liu,Ye Wang,Yuxin Chen,Fengjun Li,Bo Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PhantomSeal提出一种主动式人脸换脸防护与溯源框架：通过向图像嵌入隐蔽的“披风身份”特征，使后续换脸生成偏向该预设身份，从源头降低目标身份被成功替换的概率，并支持对篡改内容进行特征级取证识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测与溯源方法大多在伪造内容生成后进行识别，属于事后防御，难以及时阻止原始图像被用于换脸。论文旨在构建可在图像发布前部署的主动防护机制，同时保护人物身份及图像语境，并保留可追踪的取证能力。

Method: 方法核心为新型图像伪装（cloaking）技术：在原始人像中注入经过优化的、视觉上隐蔽的预设身份标识。该标识会干扰换脸模型对源身份特征的提取与迁移，使生成模型在换脸结果中更趋向于预选的披风身份，而非攻击者期望替换的真实身份。同时，系统利用该嵌入身份产生的特征痕迹，对疑似篡改图像进行识别和溯源。

Result: 实验覆盖多种人脸换脸架构与模型，结果表明PhantomSeal具备较好的有效性与鲁棒性。以SimSwap为例，其将攻击成功率降至0.30%，并能正确识别97.97%的被篡改内容，说明该方案可显著削弱换脸攻击效果，并维持较高的取证检测准确率。

Conclusion: PhantomSeal将身份保护、图像语境保护和取证追踪整合到统一的主动防御方案中，相比纯后验检测更接近源头治理。其贡献在于利用可控身份特征伪装来重定向换脸生成过程；实验结果显示该思路具有较强实用潜力，但实际部署仍需进一步评估其对图像质量、不同生成模型适应性及对抗性规避策略的影响。

Abstract: Deepfakes, especially face-swapping attacks, pose significant challenges to authenticity, security, and ethics across science, engineering, and society. While most existing detection/tracing approaches operate post hoc, proactive defenses that aim to intervene before deepfake generation remain limited in terms of real-world effectiveness. In this paper, we present PhantomSeal, the first proactive defense to simultaneously protect both the identity and the context of users' images from being used in face-swapping attacks, while supporting forensic tracing. We present a novel cloaking technique that embeds a selected identity as a stealthy identifier. This mechanism steers the deepfake generation process toward producing content that resembles the chosen cloak identity, thereby preventing successful face-swapping while enabling effective feature-based forensic analysis. The effectiveness and robustness of PhantomSeal is demonstrated in extensive experiments across different face-swapping architectures and models. For example, it reduces the attack success rate of SimSwap, an advanced deepfake model, to 0.30%, and correctly identifies 97.97% of manipulated content. Codes can be found at https://github.com/LiangqinRen/PhantomSeal

</details>


### [2] [AuthProbe: Specification-Driven, Multi-Identity Detection of Broken Object-Level Authorization in Recruitment API](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20574)
*Jay Barach*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出 AuthProbe：一种基于 OpenAPI 规范、使用多个受控身份进行黑盒测试的开源 API 扫描器，用于检测 BOLA/IDOR 越权访问漏洞，并支持在 CI 中自动化拦截高风险问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BOLA/IDOR 是 API 中常见且影响严重的授权缺陷。由于攻击请求在字节层面可能与正常请求完全一致，传统 Web 应用防火墙及仅使用单一身份的扫描器难以识别跨身份对象访问。论文旨在提供可自动化、可验证且适用于持续集成流程的检测方法。

Method: AuthProbe 根据 OpenAPI 规范生成和驱动请求，在两个或以上由测试操作者控制的身份下分别发现其可合法访问的对象；随后以身份 A 访问身份 B 所拥有的对象，并将响应与对象真实所有者的基准响应进行比对，以确认是否存在数据泄露。工具还遍历可预测标识符以发现枚举风险，并报告缺失认证与对象存在性预言机问题。扫描结果以机器可读报告输出，并依据严重性阈值返回退出码。

Result: 在复现某招聘 API 数据暴露漏洞类别的合成测试环境中，AuthProbe 检出了全部预置的跨身份读取漏洞；在加固版本中未产生误报。实验还表明，其运行时间随待测对象数量线性增长。工具以 Apache 2.0 许可证发布，并包含授权使用限制措施。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，多身份、基于对象所有权对照的黑盒测试能够有效弥补单身份扫描与流量特征检测在 BOLA/IDOR 发现上的不足。AuthProbe 的 OpenAPI 驱动方式、响应验证机制及 CI 集成能力，使其具备实际工程应用价值；但摘要中的评估主要基于合成 API，后续仍需在更多真实 API、复杂授权模型和写操作场景中验证其泛化性。

Abstract: Broken Object-Level Authorization (BOLA), also known as Insecure Direct Object Reference (IDOR), has topped the OWASP API Security ranking since 2019 and is the root cause of some of the largest exposures of applicant data in recruitment technology. The defining feature of this flaw class is that a malicious request is byte-for-byte indistinguishable from a legitimate one, which is precisely why web application firewalls and single identity scanners fail to catch it. We present AuthProbe, an open-source, black-box scanner that detects BOLA and IDOR in HTTP APIs by driving its tests from an OpenAPI specification and by acting under two or more identities that the operator controls. AuthProbe discovers, for each identity, the objects that identity legitimately owns, then attempts to read one identity's objects while authenticated as another and confirms a leak by comparing the response against a ground-truth fetch by the true owner. It also walks predictable identifiers to expose enumeration and reports missing authentication and existence oracles. The tool returns a severity-thresholded exit code and machine-readable reports so that it can gate a continuous integration build. On a synthetic recruitment API in which the McHire failure class is reproduced, AuthProbe detects every planted cross-identity read with no false positives on a hardened counterpart, and its running time grows linearly with the number of objects under test. AuthProbe is released under the Apache 2.0 license with an authorized-use guardrail.

</details>


### [3] [Deepfake News Detection: A Multimodal Framework Integrating LipNet, DeepSpeech and ResNET for Enhanced Audio-Visual Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20579)
*Ameena Khan,Muhammad Ahsan Aziz,Muhammad Junaid Asif,Naeem Akhter,Rana Fayyaz Ahmad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于深度伪造视频新闻检测的多模态框架，联合建模唇部运动、语音内容与人脸视频帧特征，并通过多种分类器进行真实性判别。在FakeAVCeleb数据集上，采用增强音频特征时取得94%的准确率，优于所比较的多模态集成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量伪造视频与克隆语音的生成门槛降低，使仅依赖单一视觉或音频线索的检测方法容易失效。论文的核心动机是利用音视频之间的同步性、语义一致性及人脸外观线索，构建更鲁棒的视频真实性检测机制。

Method: 方法包含三个特征分支：其一，使用LipNet编码唇部运动特征；其二，使用DeepSpeech2提取语音内容相关表示；其三，通过BlazeFace定位人脸，再使用ResNet18提取面部视觉特征。三类特征向量被拼接为统一的视频表示，随后分别输入随机森林、MLP和LSTM等机器学习与深度学习分类器进行判别。文中还引入增强后的音频特征，并以FakeAVCeleb作为实验数据集。

Result: 实验表明，该框架在FakeAVCeleb上最高达到94%的准确率，且该结果对应增强音频特征配置。作者声称其性能超过现有的多模态集成基线，说明音频增强与多模态特征融合对检测效果具有积极作用。摘要未报告精确率、召回率、F1值、AUC、类别分布、统计显著性、跨数据集泛化能力及不同分类器的独立结果，因此对性能稳定性和实际部署价值仍难以作完整判断。

Conclusion: 该研究的主要贡献是将唇动、语音和人脸外观特征进行融合，用于深度伪造视频检测；其结果支持多模态融合优于单一模态或既有基线的判断。方法设计具有一定实用性，但证据主要局限于单一数据集和准确率指标。后续应补充跨数据集测试、未知伪造方法测试、消融实验、鲁棒性评估，以及更全面的错误分析，以验证模型是否真正学习到音视频一致性而非数据集特定偏差。

Abstract: Deepfake news refers to AI-generated (or AI ma-nipulated) multimedia content intentionally generated to deceive audiences by manipulating the facial expressions, or speech while maintaining the realistic appearance. The rapid progress of generative AI has made the synthesis of highly realistic fake videos and cloned voices widely accessible, posing a serious threat to the authenticity of digital news media. This paper presents a multi-modal framework that discerns the authenticity of video content by jointly exploiting audio and visual cues, thereby addressing the challenge of detecting the deepfake videos. We proposed a framework that involves features extraction from lip movements, audio content and video frames. Lip movements and speech content are encoded using the LipNet and DeepSpeech2 models, while facial features are extracted by leveraging the use of BlazeFace and represented with ResNet18. The extracted feature vectors are concatenated into a holistic video representation and classified with an ensemble of machine learning and deep learning models, including Random Forest (RF), Multi-layer Perceptron (MLP) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks. Exten-sive experiments performed on the FakeAVCeleb dataset shows that the proposed approach attains an accuracy of 94% using augmented audio features, outperforming a state-of-the-art multi-modal ensemble baseline. The results confirm the robustness and practical potential of the proposed framework for deepfake news detection.

</details>


### [4] [Geometric Configurations of Perturbed Jailbreak Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20581)
*Lynn Delcon,Andres Algaba,Vincent Ginis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究字符串级扰动的越狱输入在多种小型指令模型内部表征中的分布特征，发现所选表征空间无法稳定地区分拒答与合规回答，仅少数下一词元与合规标签存在显著关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着提示扰动方法持续演化，模型安全机制可能被绕过。作者希望检验模型内部表征是否存在可用于识别此类输入或预测模型行为的线性结构。

Method: 在多个 Qwen 与 Llama 指令模型中，考察两类表征：最后一层最后一个词元的嵌入，以及概率最高的 50 个下一词元构成的概率空间。作者比较这些空间对不同提示形式及回答标签的分离能力，并搜索可区分行为的超平面与显著相关词元。

Result: 最后词元嵌入主要按拼写和格式差异分离输入；前 50 下一词元概率空间近似一维，但聚类现象较复杂。在以拒答为主的数据中，两种空间均未发现可有效区分行为标签的超平面。仅 Qwen-1.5B 中的“Sure”，以及 Llama-1B 中的“,”和“ĊĊ”，与合规标签呈显著关联。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，针对所测模型和数据，仅依靠末层单点嵌入或局部下一词元分布，难以构建稳健的行为判别器。少量词元相关性可能反映局部生成倾向，但不足以证明存在通用、可线性分离的内部安全表征。

Abstract: Perturbation techniques that turn unsuccessful jailbreak prompts into successful ones are continuously evolving, constituting a major security threat to LLM safety. In this paper, we investigate the internal representations of such string-level perturbed jailbreak inputs in the small weight models of the Qwen-2.5-1.5B/-3B/-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.2-1B/-3B/-3.1-8B-Instruct families. We select two representation spaces: the last-layer-last-token embedding space and the top-50 next-token probability space. The former space separates prompts based on their spelling and format, while the latter space is effectively one-dimensional but appears more complex to cluster. Within our refusal-dominated answer set we find no behavioral hyperplane in either space. Only the next token "Sure" in the 1.5B Qwen model, and both tokens "," and "ĊĊ" in the 1$ Llama model, display a significant association with a compliant-labeled answer.

</details>


### [5] [Enhancing Attack Detection Capabilities in BACnet/IP Networks Using Machine-Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20686)
*Derek Manzella,John D. Hastings*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文面向BACnet/IP楼宇自动化系统的攻击检测，改进Zeek BACnet解析日志，构建带逐包标签的仿真HVAC测试床，并评估五种无监督异常检测模型。One-Class SVM整体最佳，平均F1为0.864；高流量DoS与侦察攻击检测效果极佳，但隐蔽的属性篡改和虚假数据注入较难检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BACnet/IP承载楼宇关键控制功能，但现有防御工具、面向BACnet攻击的标注数据集及可直接用于机器学习的特征数据均较匮乏。研究旨在降低BACnet流量分析和攻击检测的工程门槛，并量化无监督模型对不同攻击类型的适用性。

Method: 作者修改CISA的Zeek BACnet解析器，输出统一的逐数据包日志以支持特征工程；使用bacpypes3构建小型商业HVAC仿真环境，结合基于物理规律的设备行为、按时间表运行的控制逻辑及逐包攻击标签生成数据集；随后以正常流量训练并测试五种无监督异常检测模型，在六类BACnet攻击场景下采用F1分数进行比较。

Result: One-Class SVM取得最强综合表现，六类攻击的平均F1分数为0.864。对于高流量的拒绝服务和侦察类攻击，F1分数超过0.99，说明流量规模和通信模式变化显著时，无监督检测器具有很高灵敏度。相比之下，属性篡改和虚假数据注入等低显著性、语义层面的攻击F1约为0.77，暴露出仅依赖包级异常特征的局限。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了BACnet协议解析、仿真数据生成和无监督检测评测的一体化基础，为楼宇自动化网络安全研究提供了可复用路径。结论表明，无监督模型尤其是One-Class SVM适合作为高流量攻击的初筛手段；但要提升对隐蔽篡改与虚假数据注入的检测，应进一步引入设备物理状态、控制逻辑一致性、时序关联和过程语义等上下文特征。

Abstract: Building Automation Systems (BAS) manage critical building functions using protocols such as BACnet/IP, yet defenders have limited tooling and few labeled datasets for detecting BACnet-specific attacks. This work addresses these gaps through three contributions. First, CISA's Zeek BACnet parser is modified to produce a unified per-packet log, simplifying feature engineering for machine-learning (ML) pipelines. Second, a simulated BACnet/IP testbed is developed using bacpypes3 to model a small commercial HVAC system with physics-based device behavior, schedule-aware controller logic, and per-packet attack labeling. Third, five unsupervised anomaly detection models are evaluated using baseline traffic and six BACnet attack types, including denial of service, reconnaissance, property tampering, and false data injection. Results show that One-Class SVM achieved the strongest overall performance, with an average F1 score of 0.864 across all attacks and F1 scores above 0.99 for high-volume denial-of-service and reconnaissance attacks. Detection is much stronger for high-volume attacks, such as DoS attacks and reconnaissance, than stealthier techniques such as tampering and false data injection, which scored around 77%.

</details>


### [6] [Buzz to Boom: Detecting Message Progression Vulnerabilities in Electron Applications via Segmented Directed Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20698)
*Jianjia Yu,Zhengyu Liu,Ziyang Li,Yu Sun,Yinzhi Cao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Proton，一种面向Electron跨进程消息传递漏洞（MPV）的分段定向模糊测试框架。该方法在589个真实Electron应用中发现23个零日漏洞，其中22个可导致操作系统命令执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Electron应用由多个权限不同的进程构成，攻击者可控数据可经由进程间消息传递传播至高权限接口。现有Electron安全研究主要关注不安全配置和恶意DOM内容，难以发现需跨多个进程、分多步触发的消息传播漏洞。

Method: Proton将端到端模糊测试按进程间消息边界划分为多个测试段。每个段的目标为：在当前进程到达危险汇点，或将测试载荷通过消息传播至下一进程。对于后者，生成的消息会作为下一段的语料库种子；随后，框架综合各进程中的崩溃输入，以验证完整的端到端利用链。

Result: 在589个真实世界Electron应用上的评估发现23个零日MPV，其中22个可造成操作系统命令执行。受影响项目包含GitHub星标超过5万的项目。作者已进行负责任披露，截至论文所述时间获得13次致谢、11个修复和11个CVE，并获得Vercel漏洞奖励。

Conclusion: Proton表明，按消息传递边界进行分段定向模糊测试能够有效覆盖Electron应用中传统方法难以处理的跨进程、多阶段漏洞路径。研究结果说明MPV在实际生态中具有较高普遍性和严重影响，跨进程消息流应成为Electron安全审计与测试的重点。

Abstract: Electron is a popular framework for building cross-platform desktop applications using web technologies. Such applications consist of multiple processes with different privilege levels that communicate via message passing. When inter-process messages carry attacker-controlled inputs, they can propagate across processes and reach privileged APIs, e.g., command execution. Such a message propagation behavior is characterized as Message Progression Vulnerabilities (MPVs). The exploitation of MPVs is challenging because it often requires multiple steps, e.g., first arbitrary code execution in one process via message passing, and then command injection in another process using another message crafted in the first process. To our knowledge, existing works on Electron security only study unsafe configurations and malicious Document Object Model (DOM) content, i.e., they cannot detect or exploit these vulnerabilities that need to be triggered by complex cross-process exploits via message passing. We present Proton, a segmented directed fuzzing framework for detecting MPVs. Our key insight is to decompose end-to-end fuzzing into per-process segments along message-passing boundaries, where the goals of fuzzing each segment are either: (i) reaching a sink in the current process or (ii) propagating the payload to the next process, to enable the exploration of another process. In the second case, the messages seed the corpus of the next segment. Finally, Proton synthesizes crash inputs from each process to validate end-to-end exploits. We evaluate Proton against 589 real-world Electron applications, resulting in 23 zero-day MPVs. Among them, 22 lead to OS command execution, including projects with over 50k GitHub stars. We responsibly disclosed all findings. To date, we have received 13 acknowledgments, 11 fixes, and 11 CVEs, including a bug bounty from Vercel.

</details>


### [7] [Evaluating Large Language Models for Symbolic Security Protocol Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20712)
*Paolo Modesti,Syed Ahmed,Ioannis Sfyrakis,Derek Enodolomwanyi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究评估GPT与DeepSeek在安全协议验证中的表现。LLM无法替代ProVerif和OFMC等形式化工具，但可作为初步筛查工具；其对保密性目标较有效，对认证目标表现较差，且置信度不能反映结论正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全协议验证通常依赖形式化验证工具，但其使用门槛和分析成本较高。研究旨在检验LLM是否能够以接近形式化工具的方式识别协议中的安全目标违例，并分析聊天模式与推理模式之间的性能差异。

Method: 作者在130个经混淆的AnB/AnBx协议上测试GPT和DeepSeek的聊天与推理模式，覆盖388项安全目标，并进行三次重复运行。模型结论以ProVerif和OFMC的结果为参照，评估召回率、精确率、F1值、跨运行稳定性及自报置信度与正确性之间的关系。

Result: 聊天模式具有较高召回率（69%至81%），但精确率低于31%，即存在大量误报。推理模式提高了精确率：GPT为66.5%，DeepSeek为45.4%，但只能检出略多于一半的问题。DeepSeek在相同基础模型下，推理机制使精确率从27.2%升至45.4%。认证目标最难处理，推理模型对注入式和非注入式一致性目标的检出率均不足一半；聊天模型则倾向于过度报告。保密性目标是例外，推理模式的F1最高达95.7%。GPT结果在运行间有89.7%的目标一致，DeepSeek为74.0%。两类模型均表现出普遍过高的自信，且置信度与正确性几乎无关。

Conclusion: LLM在该基准上未达到形式化验证工具的可靠性，尤其不适合独立完成认证性质验证。推理模式能减少误报，但会牺牲检出能力；聊天模式则更适合高召回的粗筛。实际应用中，LLM最多可用于预筛查、辅助定位和生成待验证假设，最终结论仍应由ProVerif、OFMC等形式化工具确认。

Abstract: Security protocol verification relies on formal tools such as ProVerif and OFMC. This study evaluates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can perform comparable analysis. We test GPT and DeepSeek in chat and reasoning modes over three runs on 130 obfuscated AnB/AnBx protocols covering 388 security goals, scored against ProVerif and OFMC. Chat models reach 69 to 81% recall at precision below 31%. Reasoning models reverse this trade-off, reaching 66.5% precision for GPT and 45.4% for DeepSeek, but detect just over half the attacks. DeepSeek's two modes share one underlying model, so the comparison isolates reasoning itself, which raises precision from 27.2% to 45.4%. The GPT contrast spans a model-version change and is only suggestive. All models perform worst on authentication goals: reasoning models detect well under half of injective and non-injective agreement attacks, whereas chat models over-flag them at low precision. Confidentiality is the exception, with F1 up to 95.7% in reasoning mode. Verdicts are unstable across runs, identical on 89.7% of goals for GPT but 74.0% for DeepSeek. Self-reported confidence is uniformly high yet shows no meaningful correlation with correctness. On this benchmark LLMs do not match formal verification, but may serve, at best, as pre-screening filters.

</details>


### [8] [Security Vulnerability Patterns in AI-Generated Code: A Cross-Model Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20713)
*Shanna M. Kahn,John D. Hastings*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究比较了三种大语言模型生成的自动化脚本安全性，发现所有脚本均含可利用漏洞，且不同模型之间的总体风险差异很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非专业用户可借助大语言模型快速生成自动化脚本，这些代码可能在缺乏充分安全审查的情况下进入企业工作流，因此需要评估其实际安全风险及风险来源。

Method: 研究使用相同提示词，让ChatGPT、Microsoft Copilot和Google Gemini在三个自动化领域生成代码；随后采用Claude Code进行标准化漏洞审查，将发现的问题按CVSS v3.1评分，并映射至OWASP Top 10:2021和MITRE ATT&CK框架。

Result: 全部生成脚本均发现可利用漏洞。共识别17类漏洞，其中9类出现在全部三个模型的代码中，14类至少出现在两个模型中；各平台的加权CVSS评分差异不足10%。

Conclusion: 自动化脚本的安全风险主要与任务类别有关，而非由某一特定模型单独决定。组织不应仅讨论应信任哪种工具，而应建立对LLM生成自动化代码的人工审查、测试与安全治理机制，避免未经审查直接部署。

Abstract: LLM-based coding tools enable non-expert users to generate routine automation scripts that may enter enterprise workflows without meaningful security review. This study examines that risk directly. Code was collected from ChatGPT, Microsoft Copilot, and Google Gemini using identical prompts across three automation domains. Claude Code performed a standardized vulnerability review. Each identified vulnerability was scored using CVSS v3.1 and mapped to the OWASP Top 10:2021 and the MITRE ATT&CK frameworks. Every script contained exploitable vulnerabilities. Nine of the 17 identified vulnerability classes appeared in code from all three models, while 14 of the 17 vulnerability classes appeared in at least two models. The weighted CVSS scores across platforms differed by less than 10%. The risk is not tied to any particular model but rather to the task category. Organizations should therefore ask not which tool to trust, but instead whether LLM-generated automation code should be deployed without review.

</details>


### [9] [Leaky Language Models: Stealing Architecture and Inference Optimizations via Per-Token Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20723)
*Sadegh Majidi,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Kazem Taram*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出LeakyLMs：一种仅通过远程API返回的逐令牌生成时延，推断生产级语言模型部署优化与模型架构信息的侧信道分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭源模型服务通常不公开底层架构、推理优化和部署配置，但这些信息可能因时延模式而泄露。论文旨在验证并量化这种基于生成时序的信息泄露风险。

Method: 方法包含两类分析：其一，通过观测不同输入条件下的令牌生成延迟，识别推测解码等推理优化，并估计草稿模型的上下文窗口；其二，建立现代NVIDIA GPU上令牌生成时延与模型配置、硬件参数之间的预测模型，再在候选架构空间中搜索层数、隐藏维度和注意力头数等配置。

Result: 实验表明，该方法可检测是否采用推测解码，并可估计相关草稿模型的上下文长度。对于Llama系列模型，接近真实架构的候选配置在超过90%的实验中进入前10个猜测。文中还报告对某商业模型服务推断出其采用推测解码，且草稿上下文窗口约为128K令牌。

Conclusion: 逐令牌时延可构成显著的远程侧信道，使闭源语言模型的部署策略和关键架构属性面临泄露风险。服务提供方应将时延特征视为敏感元数据，并考虑通过时延扰动、批处理策略调整、输出节奏统一化等方式降低可推断性。

Abstract: This work presents LeakyLMs, a set of attacks that leak proprietary model, architecture, and deployment information from production language models. LeakyLMs is the first to demonstrate that key model and deployment details can be inferred using only token generation timing, even when interacting through remote APIs. LeakyLMs introduces two core attacks. The first attack targets inference optimizations and deployment strategies. For example, our attack detects whether a provider uses speculative decoding, a widely deployed inference-time optimization, and further identifies the context length of the draft model used in the pipeline. Our measurements show that Google Gemini Flash 2.5 uses speculative decoding with a draft context window of approximately 128K tokens. The second attack recovers key architectural properties, including the number of transformer layers, hidden dimension size, and number of attention heads. To achieve this, LeakyLMs builds a detailed and accurate model of token-generation timing on modern NVIDIA GPUs, characterizing how latency scales with model configuration and hardware parameters. The attack then performs a search over the architecture space using this timing model. In experiments with Llama models, the near-correct architectural configuration appears in the top-10 guesses more than 90% of the time.

</details>


### [10] [GPE: Evaluating Robust Evidence Aggregation for Fact Verification under Controllable GEO-Style Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20730)
*Zhaoqi Wang,Zijian Zhang,Xiaomei Yuan,Pengtao Kou,Jiamou Liu,Zhen Li,Liehuang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出GPE，一个用于评估大语言模型在受操纵检索证据环境下事实核查鲁棒性的多领域基准与评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用搜索工具的大语言模型依赖外部检索信息，而生成式引擎优化可能提升特定内容被检索、引用和采纳的概率，使证据投毒成为新的风险。现有事实核查基准通常基于静态、干净的证据环境，无法系统控制证据来源及投毒比例，也难以揭示模型在对抗性检索环境中的脆弱性。

Method: 作者构建GPE，包括多领域事实核查任务集，以及可控配置证据来源和投毒比例的评估框架。该框架通过向检索证据池注入不同程度、不同形式的误导性内容，在统一条件下测试多种事实核查方法，并比较其在干净与受污染环境中的准确性、鲁棒性和效率表现。

Result: 实验表明，多种事实核查方法在证据投毒条件下均出现不同程度的鲁棒性下降；同时，部分更具防御性的策略需要付出效率代价。仅在干净证据集上进行评测无法观察到这些性能退化和准确率—效率权衡。

Conclusion: GPE证明事实核查系统应在可控的对抗性证据环境中进行评估，而不应只依赖传统干净基准。该工作为研究检索增强大语言模型面对证据操纵时的可靠性，以及设计更稳健的验证方法，提供了标准化测试基础。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly use search tools to retrieve up-to-date information, introducing a new attack surface in which retrieved documents can be manipulated. This risk is amplified by the development of generative engine optimization, which can make selected content more likely to be retrieved, cited, and adopted by models. Existing fact-verification benchmarks and evaluation frameworks do not provide the controlled evidence environments needed to assess robustness against GEO poisoning. We therefore propose GPE, which consists of a multi-domain fact-verification benchmark and an evaluation framework for controlling evidence sources and poisoning ratios. Experiments across multiple verification methods and poisoning attacks demonstrate that GPE exposes robustness degradation and efficiency trade-offs that cannot be observed through clean evaluation alone, confirming the need to evaluate fact verification under adversarial evidence environments.

</details>


### [11] [IssueTrojanBench: Benchmarking AI Coding Agents Against Malicious Issue Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20759)
*Ankur Singh,Jinqiu Yang,Tse-Hsun Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了真实部署的AI编程代理面对恶意议题请求时的安全性，发现大量攻击可绕过代理与模型两层防护，现有代理级防御增益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程代理具备读写本地文件、调用工具和执行代码的能力。除语言模型本身可能受对抗提示、训练数据污染或后门影响外，代理式工具调用还可能扩大外部接口滥用、数据泄露和开发环境持续受控等风险，因此需要在真实产品中量化此类风险。

Method: 作者构建IssueTrojanBench基准，将恶意指令嵌入软件开发议题中，并设计四类攻击类别、六种投递载体（如PDF和议题评论）及多种扰动方式。实验评测三种编程代理：Cursor、Claude Code与Codex Desktop；底层覆盖GPT-5.3 Codex、GPT-5.4和Sonnet 4.6等模型，并分析拒绝行为来自模型层还是代理框架层。

Result: 66.5%的恶意议题能够穿透所评测编程代理的代理级与模型级防护。拒绝主要由底层语言模型产生，代理框架本身几乎未提供显著额外拦截能力。GPT系列模型整体更易受攻击；Sonnet 4.6对高影响操作表现出更具选择性、风险感知的阻断能力。

Conclusion: 当前AI编程代理的安全边界明显依赖底层模型，单纯的代理级防护不足以抵御经多种载体伪装的恶意任务。论文建议同时强化模型层的指令安全与代理层的权限控制、工具调用审查、上下文可信度验证及高风险操作确认机制。

Abstract: AI coding agents powered by LLMs are increasingly integrated into real-world software development, where they generate, edit, and execute code with autonomous access to local files and tools. Coding agents inherit security risks from both the LLM backbone, where adversarial prompts, poisoned training data, and backdoor triggers can cause models to emit insecure or attacker-chosen code, and their agentic architecture, where tool-using autonomy enables induced misuse of external APIs, data exfiltration, and persistent compromise of development environments. This paper presents a systematic evaluation of malicious issue requests against state-of-the-art coding agents (Cursor, Claude Code, and Codex Desktop), powered by two major model families (OpenAI GPT-5.3 Codex/GPT-5.4 and Anthropic Sonnet 4.6). Our novel benchmark IssueTrojanBench contains malicious issues that are constructed based on four novel attack categories (i.e., embedded as malicious instructions in issues), six delivery vectors (e.g., PDF, or issue comment), and further augmented by perturbations. Our results reveal critical vulnerabilities in the as-deployed modern coding agents, i.e., 66.5% of the malicious issues from IssueTrojanBench penetrate all the guardrails (agent- and LLM-level) of coding agents. Our further analysis shows that rejection is almost entirely from LLMs rather than the agent frameworks, with GPT models broadly vulnerable and Sonnet 4.6 exhibiting more selective, risk-aware blocking of high-impact actions. Our evaluation also highlights that the current agent-level defense strategy offers limited additional protection for coding agents. Our findings highlight the urgent need for stronger agent- and model-level safety mechanisms to protect AI coding agents.

</details>


### [12] [Classical Acceptance Is Not Hybrid Authentication: Measuring X.509 Verifier Semantics in Post-Quantum Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20800)
*Taesung Kim,Boheung Chung,Keonwoo Kim,Yousung Kang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出：混合X.509证书即使同时携带经典密码凭证与后量子凭证，现有验证器也可能仅依据经典证书路径作出接受决定，却将结果错误地表述为“混合验证成功”。作者在多种实现、模式和证书方案中测得该结构性缺陷，并提出可执行的策略化验证契约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合证书旨在兼容既有公钥基础设施，同时提供经典与后量子密码学的联合保障。然而，可分离式设计通常将后量子证据放置在传统路径验证可忽略的位置。若验证结果未明确要求后量子证据参与接受判定，则后量子凭证即使失效、撤销或未被正确绑定，系统仍可能接受经典路径，从而使“混合”安全声明缺乏实际依据。

Method: 作者基于规范推导验证器模型，定义混合验证必须满足的步骤：识别后量子证据、验证其密码学有效性、将其验证结果纳入接受决策、检查经典与后量子凭证之间的绑定关系，并仅在全部条件满足时报告混合验证成功。实验覆盖8个路径验证栈、7个独立代码库、9种验证模式及6类证书方案；同时考察后量子凭证被撤销而经典证书仍有效的生命周期不同步场景。

Result: 在要求混合验证的策略下，几乎所有能够解析可分离混合证书的验证栈都会沿经典路径接受证书，而不会使后量子证据成为决定性条件。一个强制执行模式则因签名输入编码尚未形成可互操作的规范而导致互操作性破裂。即使部分栈具备后量子签名验证能力，也通常不会默认强制绑定与结果依赖关系。生命周期不同步实验进一步表明：当已绑定的后量子凭证被撤销、经典证书仍有效时，默认验证路径仍会接受。

Conclusion: 问题的根源并非单纯缺少后量子密码原语支持，而是标准与实现未要求后量子证据必须进入最终接受判定的结构性缺口。成功完成凭证绑定不等于完成混合认证。论文提出的参考契约可作为实现和策略评估基线：只有后量子证据被识别、验证、绑定检查且其结果对接受决定具有实际约束力时，验证器才应声明混合验证成功。

Abstract: A relying party validating a hybrid X.509 certificate --- carrying both a classical and a post-quantum credential --- must distinguish whether its accepting judgment rests on the post-quantum evidence or only on the classical path. To preserve compatibility, the separable designs place that evidence where classical path validation may ignore it. A verifier can then validate the classical path and accept while the post-quantum evidence never bears on the decision --- a valid classical result silently promoted to a hybrid conclusion it did not establish. We measure this across eight path-validation stacks (seven independent codebases), in nine validation modes, over six certificate schemes. Under a hybrid-required policy, nearly every stack parsing a separable hybrid certificate accepts on the classical path without making the post-quantum evidence outcome-bearing; one enforcing mode instead fractures interoperability over a signature-input encoding not yet interoperably profiled; and stacks that verify post-quantum signatures still do not enforce the binding by default: the gap is structural, not explained by missing primitive capability alone. Under lifecycle desynchronization the downgrade is realized: when a bound post-quantum credential is revoked while the classical certificate stays valid, the default path still accepts, because the bound credential lies outside the decision's scope. Binding success is not authentication success. We contribute a specification-derived verifier model and an executable, policy-parametric reference contract --- what a verifier must recognize, verify, make outcome-bearing, and check before reporting a validation as hybrid --- with a diagnosis of why standards do not require it.

</details>


### [13] [Which Model Is Actually Serving You? IRIS: Budgeted Black-Box Auditing of Model Substitution and Routing Dilution in LLM Gateways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20860)
*Yuewei Zhang,Zhi-Hai Zhang,Hanzhang Qin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: IRIS是一种仅依赖模型返回文本的黑盒审计方法，用于验证商业LLM网关实际服务的后端是否与宣称模型一致，并识别全量替换或按比例掺入低成本模型的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒审计通常依赖对数概率、词元排名或参考样本等特权信息，或需要针对特定目标设计探针；同时往往预先固定查询预算且只给出二元结论。因此，需要一种文本可得、可归因、可估计路由比例并能自适应确定查询预算的通用审计方案。

Method: IRIS要求端点生成随机数字或随机字符串，并基于输出统计特征构建后端指纹。方法覆盖两类异常：每次请求均被替换的全流替换，以及仅以比例ε路由至替代模型的稀释。其先利用低成本预实验拟合查询数与错误率之间的指数衰减关系，在发出被审计查询前冻结所需预算；随后以累积证据完成模型检测、候选后端归因和ε估计。

Result: 在Qwen3同系列模型上，IRIS的后端验证达到0.99 AUROC，且随查询增加归因能力提升。在OpenRouter模型库中，对于满足间隔条件的模型对，检测ε=0.3稀释的平均检验功效为0.85，假阳性率为0.017；对已登记的稀释模型，ε估计误差在0.04以内。真实跨服务商实验中，15个同模型服务商配对中有14个因量化和内核差异被标记，且得到第三方MET轨迹佐证。与可比黑盒审计器相比，其在共享任务上达到或超过检测性能；自适应预算分配将相同预算下的目标命中率从73%提高到87%。

Conclusion: IRIS将文本唯一可见条件下的模型一致性检测、后端归因、混合路由比例估计和预算自适应整合为统一审计流程。实验表明，该方法对模型替换及部分流量稀释具有较强实用性，但其效果仍依赖候选模型之间存在可稳定辨识的输出指纹，以及预实验对误差衰减规律的拟合有效性。

Abstract: Commercial LLM gateways mediate access to hosted models, but the served backend may not match the advertised one: it may substitute a cheaper model on every request or route only a fraction $ε$ of requests to it. Prior black-box auditors often need a privileged signal (log-probabilities, token ranks, or reference samples) or a target-specific probe, fix the query budget in advance, and return a yes/no verdict. We present $\mathrm{IRIS}$, an audit that needs only the returned text: it asks endpoints to generate random numbers or strings, fingerprints the backend, and is the first to combine, in one text-only audit, detection of whole-stream substitution and fractional dilution, attribution of the served backend, routing-fraction ($ε$) estimation, and a query budget it sizes itself. A cheap pilot fits the exponential query-error decay and freezes that budget before any suspect query is issued. On an intra-family Qwen3 ladder $\mathrm{IRIS}$ verifies the backend at $0.99$ AUROC and sharpens attribution as queries accumulate; across a commercial OpenRouter library it catches $ε{=}0.3$ dilution on margin-qualified pairs at $0.85$ mean power ($0.017$ false-positive rate) and recovers $ε$ to within $0.04$ for enrolled diluents; and a live cross-provider audit flags $14$ of $15$ same-model provider pairs by genuine quantization and kernel deviations, corroborated on third-party MET traces. Against comparable black-box auditors, $\mathrm{IRIS}$ matches or beats detection on shared tasks, and adaptive allocation lifts the matched-budget target-hit rate from $73$% to $87$%. Further experiments cover adversarial gateways, knob identifiability, unseen diluents, and false-positive control.

</details>


### [14] [Toward cryptographically verifiable authorization for autonomous AI agents: A security hypothesis, preliminary formal model, and proof-of-concept implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21325)
*M. Llambí-Morillas,D. Fernández-Fernández*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“密码学可验证智能体授权”（CVA）框架：通过零知识证明，为特定智能体在特定执行上下文中发出的具体授权请求满足既定策略提供可验证证据，同时尽量不泄露私有授权属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有身份认证与授权委托机制能够确认主体身份或授予权限，但通常无法以密码学方式证明某一具体请求确实由指定智能体发出、符合特定策略且发生于给定运行环境。随着自主智能体可调用工具并访问受保护资源，该证据缺口会削弱可审计性、最小权限执行和责任追踪能力。

Method: 作者将授权建模为关系R_CVA，联合绑定智能体主体、具体授权请求、执行上下文及策略满足性。论文提出候选安全性质：授权健全性、主体绑定、请求绑定、策略绑定和抗重放性；并基于Groth16 zk-SNARK实现可执行的零知识概念验证。研究还明确区分身份绑定、授权请求绑定和运行时执行绑定，并将三者之间的可靠衔接定义为开放问题。

Result: 论文的主要产出是初步形式化抽象、安全属性集合及零知识原型，而非完整生产级授权系统。原型表明可利用Groth16在不公开部分私有授权属性的情况下证明选定授权条件。作者同时指出，现有智能体安全框架通常未明确处理身份、请求与实际运行时执行三种绑定之间的结构性分离。

Conclusion: CVA为智能体授权提供了从“已授予权限”走向“可验证地证明具体行为获授权”的研究方向。其价值在于将授权证据、隐私保护与策略约束结合；但关键挑战仍是确保零知识证明中的主体和请求能够可信地对应真实运行时执行。后续工作应完善执行绑定模型、威胁模型、策略表达能力、密钥与撤销机制，以及在真实多工具智能体环境中的性能和可部署性验证。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly execute actions, invoke tools, and operate on protected resources with limited human oversight. Existing authentication and authorization mechanisms establish identity and delegate authority, but do not inherently provide cryptographic evidence that a concrete request issued by a specific agent satisfies the applicable policy in a specific execution context. This paper hypothesizes that agent authorization can be formalized as a cryptographically verifiable relation, denoted $R_{CVA}$, that jointly binds an agent principal, a concrete authorization request, an execution context, and the satisfaction of an applicable policy, while selectively preserving the confidentiality of private authorization attributes. We introduce a preliminary formal abstraction for Cryptographically Verifiable Agent Authorization (CVA), define a compact set of candidate security properties including authorization soundness, principal binding, request binding, policy binding, and replay resistance, and provide an executable zero-knowledge proof of concept that instantiates selected elements of the model over a Groth16 zk-SNARK construction. We further identify and formalize the structural separation among identity binding, authorization-request binding, and runtime execution binding as a central open problem in the design of secure agentic systems (a distinction {not explicitly addressed by} current agentic security frameworks) and present a falsifiable research agenda for its resolution.

</details>


### [15] [Themis Consensus Extension v1: MEV Mitigation by Randomized Delayed Execution and Intent-Hiding Transactions in Application-Specific Blockchains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21406)
*Shoeb Siddiqui,Mateusz Nowakowski,Stanislav Vozarik,Gleb Urvanov,Peter Kris*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文系统化分析了Themis Consensus Extension v1，一种面向MEV缓解的共识扩展机制。其核心是将重排序型价值提取（VER）与拒绝服务型价值提取（VED）分离：前者通过跨连续区块生产者的承诺、随机种子和确定性洗牌限制单方排序操控；后者通过分层加密隐藏交易意图，并在提交后由执行者解密执行。论文强调该方案只能在明确假设下限制部分MEV，不能保证全面公平或完全抗审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统区块构建者可通过选择、插入、排除或重排待处理交易获取MEV。现有缓解方案常将不同类型的操控风险混为一谈，或者对公平性与抗操控能力作出过强表述。本文旨在精确定义Themis机制的安全边界、实现细节和适用假设，区分其对排序操控与选择性拒绝的缓解效果，并修正对其能力的泛化理解。

Method: 论文对Themis v1进行协议规格化与安全分析。对于VER，前一生产者先承诺交易集合，后一生产者基于公开可验证、确定且此前不可预测的随机种子，对交易进行保持依赖关系的置换后执行；实现中涉及BABE、sr25519/VRF随机种子路径、延迟执行、Fisher-Yates洗牌和Xoshiro256++随机数生成。对于选择性VED，用户将交易分层加密给指定构建者和执行者：构建者移除外层并提交内层密文，执行者在提交后解密明文并执行。分析覆盖概率性提取、垃圾交易、交易依赖、解密活性、会话边界、完全拒绝以及阈值联盟等情形，并以自利但不串谋的验证者、对手低于底层共识容错阈值、密码学安全和角色行为可追责为主要前提。

Result: 结果表明，Themis可降低单个参与者在交易集合提交之后单方面操纵执行顺序的能力，并能向中继者及构建者隐藏加密交易的业务意图。跨生产者承诺与基于随机种子的确定性置换，使事后重排难以由单一角色控制；分层加密使构建者在承诺阶段无法直接观察内层交易内容。与此同时，论文指出其保护具有条件性：随机性、角色诚实性、执行与解密活性，以及非串谋假设均是关键。

Conclusion: Themis v1并非通用MEV消除方案，而是在特定共识与密码学假设下，针对VER和选择性VED分别提供有限缓解的机制。它不保证发送顺序或接收顺序公平、不提供完整抗审查能力、无法抵抗构建者与执行者串谋，也不提供逐笔交易价格保证。论文的主要贡献在于保留原始设计思路，同时以更严格的威胁模型、边界条件和实现描述收窄其安全声明。

Abstract: Maximal extractable value (MEV) arises when privileged participants select, exclude, insert, or reorder pending transactions for private gain. We specify and analyze the Themis Consensus Extension v1, first published by Mangata in 2021. The design separates value extraction by reordering (VER) from value extraction by denial (VED). For VER, block construction and execution occur across consecutive producers: one producer commits a transaction set, and the next derives a publicly verifiable, deterministic, previously un- predictable seed and executes a seed-determined, dependency-preserving permutation. For selective VED, a user may encrypt a transaction for a designated builder and executor. The builder removes an outer layer and commits the opaque inner ciphertext; the executor reveals and executes the plaintext only after commitment. Under selfish but non-colluding validators, an adversary below the underlying consensus fault threshold, secure cryptography, and accountable role performance, the construction limits unilateral post-commit ordering control and hides transaction intent from relays and the builder. It does not provide send-order or receive-order fairness, complete censorship resistance, resistance to builder-executor collusion, or per-transaction price guarantees. We analyze probabilistic extraction, spam, dependent transactions, decryption liveness, session boundaries, total denial, and threshold coalitions. We also document the initial Aura-based Substrate implementation and its subsequent transition to a BABE-based sr25519/VRF seed path, together with delayed execution, Fisher-Yates shuffling, and Xoshiro256++. The result preserves the original proposal while narrowing its claims to explicit assumptions.

</details>


### [16] [Where You Tap Matters: A Probe-and-Model Benchmark for Open-Set RF Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21564)
*Gabriele Oligeri,Savio Sciancalepore,Ingrid Huso,Fatima Al-Mousawi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了BPSK接收机链路中不同采样探针位置对开放集、基于重构误差的射频指纹识别性能的影响。结论是：采样位置比自编码器架构复杂度更决定性能，定时恢复后的信号通常最适合该任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有射频指纹识别研究对于应在接收机处理链的何处采集信号样本缺乏一致结论。载波恢复、增益归一化、脉冲成形和定时恢复等操作既可能降低同一发射机样本的内部波动，也可能抑制用于区分设备的硬件非理想特征。因此，需要在真实环境中定量比较不同处理阶段对开放集识别能力的影响，并澄清模型结构与采样位置何者更关键。

Method: 研究在标准BPSK接收机链的5个探针点采集真实数据，采用以重构误差为判据的开放集射频指纹识别框架，比较各探针点下的真实接受率、虚假接受率及分布内与分布外样本重构误差的重叠程度。为排除模型选择带来的混杂影响，作者还在统一的受控流程中测试多个由大语言模型设计的自编码器：固定预处理方法和均方误差评分规则，仅改变网络架构，并与基线模型比较性能和训练时间。

Result: 结果表明识别性能对探针位置高度敏感。定时恢复阶段、以及较弱程度上的载波恢复阶段，能够在较低虚假接受率下实现较好的开放集识别，且分布内与分布外误差重叠较少。其他多个处理阶段则通常需要将虚假接受率提高到0.1以上，才能达到0.9的真实接受率。多种大语言模型设计的自编码器均复现了这种探针依赖性；在选定工作点上，它们没有优于基线模型，并且通常增加训练开销。

Conclusion: 对于基于重构误差的开放集射频指纹识别，接收机链中的信号采样位置是首要设计因素，影响超过自编码器网络复杂度。实践上应优先评估并选择定时恢复后或载波恢复后的探针数据，而非仅通过增加模型复杂度来追求性能提升。

Abstract: Radio Frequency Fingerprint Identification (RFFI) enables transmitter identification at the physical layer by learning device-specific impairments from received signals, yet the literature is inconsistent about where in the receiver chain those samples should be collected. Since distinct transformations are applied to the signal by the different receiver operations, i.e., carrier recovery, gain normalization, pulse shaping, and timing recovery, they can either tighten within-transmitter variability or suppress the features RFFI requires for classification. We present a systematic real-world evaluation of open-set, reconstruction-error RFFI using data collected at five probe points along a standard BPSK receiver chain. Our results show that RFFI is strongly probe-dependent: timing recovery and, to a lesser extent, carrier recovery enable low false-acceptance operation with limited in-distribution-out-of-distribution overlap, whereas other stages often require a false-acceptance ratio above 0.1 to achieve a true-acceptance ratio of 0.9. To test the validity of our findings across model selection, we benchmark several LLM-designed autoencoders using a controlled pipeline that holds preprocessing and MSE scoring fixed. These architectures confirm that RFFI is probe-dependent. Moreover, they do not outperform the baseline at the chosen operating point and typically increase training time. Overall, probe selection dominates reconstruction-based open-set RFFI performance, more than the autoencoder complexity.

</details>

### [17] [Verifier-First Evaluation of Agentic LLMs for Infrastructure-as-Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20478)
*Mohamed Jouini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究评估七种面向Terraform自然语言生成的智能体策略，核心结论是：检索、验证器反馈迭代和提示优化均能显著提升IaC任务通过率；其中GPT-4o结合迭代验证达到84.4% pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IaC生成不仅需要语法正确，还必须满足云服务商Schema、资源依赖关系和组织策略。现有评估若只关注配置表面合理性，无法定位失败发生在语法验证、执行规划还是策略合规阶段，因此需要以验证器为中心的系统比较。

Method: 作者在IaC-Eval v2的186个AWS/Terraform任务上比较七种智能体策略，并将失败分为terraform validate、terraform plan和opa eval三个阶段。统计上采用全部成对比较的McNemar检验及Wilson置信区间（alpha=0.05）。考察方法包括主动RAG、基于验证器反馈的迭代修复、GEPA反思式指令优化、SIMBA无教师示例注入，以及向模型提供Rego策略文本的诊断实验。

Result: 主动RAG将Qwen2.5-Coder 7B的pass@1从14.0%提升至45.7%，主要原因是VALIDATE_FAIL由144项降至66项。迭代验证修复使Qwen 7B达到62.9%、GPT-4o达到84.4%，且呈现“一次重试解决或耗尽预算”的二元收敛现象。GEPA在主动RAG基础上额外提升7.5个百分点（p=0.026），仅使用80次验证器引导的rollout。SIMBA与主动RAG性能相当（p=1.0），但未能缓解主要的SELF_DEFINED_PROPERTY错误。诊断实验表明，迭代修复后79%的OPA失败属于策略信息缺失：当策略文本可见时可以解决（p=0.016）。

Conclusion: 验证器反馈是提升可验证IaC生成可靠性的关键机制；检索尤其有助于减少Schema或配置验证错误，迭代修复可进一步显著提高通过率，而策略文本的可见性对解决OPA合规失败至关重要。摘要末句在“motivating policy”处截断，因此作者对策略信息注入的最终设计建议未被完整呈现。

Abstract: Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC) generation from natural language requires satisfying provider schemas, dependency planning, and organizational policy constraints, not merely producing syntactically plausible configurations. We present a verifier-first empirical study of seven agentic strategies for Terraform generation evaluated on IaC-Eval v2, a modernized 186-task AWS/Terraform benchmark with Rego v1 intent policies. Our evaluation separates failures into three verifier stages (terraform validate, terraform plan, opa eval) and applies McNemar's test with Wilson confidence intervals on all pairwise comparisons (n=186, alpha=0.05).
  We report five principal findings. (1) Active retrieval via ReAct agents with MCP or ChromaDB-backed RAG raises Qwen2.5-Coder 7B from 14.0% to 45.7% pass@1 (p<0.0001), primarily by reducing VALIDATE_FAIL from 144 to 66 tasks. (2) Iterative refinement with verifier feedback achieves 62.9% (Qwen 7B) and 84.4% (GPT-4o) pass@1, exhibiting binary convergence -- tasks either resolve in one retry or exhaust the budget. (3) GEPA reflective instruction optimization raises the Active RAG baseline by +7.5 pp (p=0.026) using only 80 verifier-guided rollouts, providing evidence that prompt optimizers can improve verifiable IaC generation without weight updates. (4) SIMBA teacher-free demonstration injection achieves performance equivalent to Active RAG (p=1.0) without retrieval infrastructure, but fails to address the dominant SELF_DEFINED_PROPERTY error class (50% of failures). (5) A diagnostic Rego-injection experiment shows that 79% of post-refinement OPA failures are information-gap failures resolvable when policy text is visible (p=0.016), motivating policy

</details>


### [18] [Transformer-Assisted LLM-Based Source Code Summarisation: to Enable More Secure Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20933)
*Jesse Phillips,Tracy Hall,Paul Rayson,Mo El-Haj*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“Transformer辅助的基于大语言模型的源代码摘要”方法：将任务专用Transformer生成的摘要作为提示信息输入大语言模型，以改善源代码自然语言摘要的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务专用Transformer在BLEU等自动指标上表现较好，但这些指标偏重词汇重叠，未必反映语义质量；大语言模型更擅长理解代码语义并生成自然摘要，却因措辞与人工参考摘要差异较大而获得较低自动指标分数。论文旨在结合两类方法的优势，并利用可在开发者工作站运行的大语言模型提升代码维护中的摘要可用性。

Method: 作者设计四种提示模板，并在提示中引入任务专用Transformer生成的候选摘要，以辅助四个大语言模型生成最终源代码摘要。该方法通过提示工程将Transformer的词汇对齐能力与大语言模型的语义生成能力结合，再使用自然语言生成指标进行比较评估。

Result: 实验观察到混合方法相较相关基线具有提升：BLEU-4提高7.8%。摘要还提到“5%”的改进，但原文在该处截断，未明确说明对应的具体评价指标、比较对象或完整数值。

Conclusion: Transformer生成的中间摘要可作为有效提示上下文，帮助大语言模型在保持较强语义表达能力的同时，提高与参考摘要的词汇匹配程度。该研究表明，Transformer与大语言模型的协同方式是源代码摘要的一种可行方向；但其实际摘要质量仍应结合人工评估、语义一致性和事实正确性等指标验证，而不宜仅依据BLEU等词汇重叠指标判断。

Abstract: Neural Source Code Summarisation (NSCS) aims to generate natural language summaries of source code to improve developers' and maintainers' understanding of code. Source code summaries are vital during the maintenance phase of the Secure Software Development Lifecycle (SSDLC), as they improve maintainers' understanding of code and help reduce the number of bugs and vulnerabilities in a software system. However, summaries are often missing, incomplete, or outdated in many software systems. Solutions to this problem use small, task-specific Transformer models or code-aware Large Language Models (LLMs). Task-specific Transformer-generated summaries often score well across many natural language generation (NLG) metrics, but these metrics reward lexical overlap rather than summary quality. Conversely, the ability of LLMs to capture semantics and produce high-quality summaries presents an exciting solution to this problem. This is especially relevant given the increased availability of LLMs and improvements in workstation hardware in recent years, which mean that some LLMs can now be run on developers' workstations. However, because of their abstractive nature, LLM-generated code summaries often differ greatly from developer-written summaries in the words and phrases they use, resulting in low scores across NLG metrics. We show how combining these two methods, by using Transformer-generated summaries in prompt engineering, may enable LLMs to create better source code summaries and help software practitioners maintain secure systems. We prompt four LLMs using four different prompts, with a task-specific Transformer used to assist the LLMs within the prompts. We present "Transformer-Assisted LLM-Based Source Code Summarisation", a method through which we observe an improvement of 7.8% in BLEU-4 and 5%.

</details>


### [19] [Maintenance Signals in AI-Assisted GitHub Repositories: Evidence from GenAI Adopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21079)
*Rikuto Tsuchida,Youmei Fan,Kazumasa Shimari,Raula Gaikovina Kula,Gema Rodríguez-Pérez,Kenichi Matsumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析公开采用生成式人工智能辅助开发的GitHub用户、仓库及问题记录，发现AI辅助编程并未消除维护成本，而是可能将成本转移至文档编写、生成内容验证、调试、外部依赖管理和AI特定行为测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能能够降低代码生成工作量，但其对软件长期维护成本的影响尚不明确。研究旨在识别AI辅助开发者在公开协作平台上可观测的维护成本信号，并比较AI辅助仓库与传统仓库的差异。

Method: 研究分析了622名公开表明采用生成式人工智能的用户、179个具有可见AI辅助配置文件的仓库、179个匹配的传统仓库，以及248个AI辅助仓库创建的问题记录。研究通过比较仓库文档特征，并对与生成式人工智能相关的问题主题进行分析，识别维护活动与依赖风险。

Result: AI辅助仓库覆盖多种项目类型，其README通常更长，包含更多标题和代码块；传统仓库则包含更多外部链接。与生成式人工智能技术相关的问题经常涉及外部依赖，例如API速率限制以及对AI服务提供方接口的依赖。

Conclusion: AI辅助开发的维护负担呈现结构性转移：开发者需要投入更多工作以验证生成内容、维护和管理外部AI服务依赖，并测试AI相关功能的正确性与稳定性。因此，评估AI编程工具的效益时，应同时考虑代码生成效率与后续维护成本。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) can reduce code-generation effort, but it may shift work to documentation, validation, debugging, and maintenance. We study observable maintenance-cost signals among GenAI adopters on GitHub by analyzing 622 users who publicly signal adoption, 179 repositories with visible AI-assistance configuration files, 179 matched traditional repositories, and 248 issues created in AI-assisted repositories. AI-assisted repositories span diverse project types and contain longer README files with more headers and code blocks, while traditional repositories contain more external URLs. Issues concerning GenAI technology often involve external dependencies, such as API rate limits and reliance on GenAI provider APIs. These findings suggest that AI assistance shifts maintenance costs toward verifying generated content, managing external AI dependencies, and validating AI-specific behavior.

</details>


### [20] [Improving Communication of Changes in Model-Based Engineering with Model-Independent Change Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21084)
*Philip Ochs,Lars Gesmann,Tobias Pett,Ina Schaefer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出将Delta建模中的形式化变更自动映射为模型无关的自然语言变更描述，以改善模型驱动工程中跨学科团队对模型演化的理解与沟通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨学科工程团队使用不同类型的模型协作时，一方对模型所作的变更通常难以被其他学科成员理解。非正式语言描述虽易读但缺乏一致性与客观性；形式化变更描述虽精确却不具备良好的可解释性。因此，需要一种兼具语义准确性、模型独立性和人类可读性的变更表达方式。

Method: 作者设计了一组映射函数，将以Delta建模方式形式化表示的变更转换为模型无关语言中的变更描述。论文采用穷尽式混合方法评估：通过实现框架定量验证技术可行性；通过案例研究评估技术适用性；并借助用户研究定性考察描述结果的合理性、实际适用性和可扩展性。

Result: 评估表明，该方法能够在实现层面生成自动化的模型无关变更描述，并可应用于案例场景。用户研究显示，所生成描述在可理解性与实践使用方面具有一定合理性，同时具备进一步扩展的潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究为模型驱动工程中的自动化、模型无关变更说明提供了一个有前景的起点。其核心贡献是连接形式化变更语义与跨学科成员可理解的语言表达，但摘要未给出具体的准确率、用户规模、对照基线或适用边界，因而实际效果仍需结合全文实验细节进一步判断。

Abstract: In model-based engineering, inter-disciplinary teams collaborate through models, which change over time for purposes of system development, what makes the proper description of such changes crucial for engineers. However, any change made by the engineer of one discipline will be difficult to understand by the engineers of other disciplines. To overcome this limitation, model-independent change descriptions can be derived instead, which preserve semantics of the changes and do not require model-specific knowledge. The two opposing approaches here are to describe changes using either informal language or formal notions of change. While informal language lacks objectivity and standardisation, formal notions of change lack human interpretability, and thus offering no support for inter-disciplinary communication. In this paper, we propose functions to map formally specified changes, represented in the approach of delta modelling, to change descriptions in model-independent language. In an exhaustive mixed-methods evaluation, we bridge the gap between the theoretical and the practical representation of changes. We quantitatively assess technical feasibility, with an implementation framework, and technical applicability, along a case study; and we qualitatively assess plausibility, practical applicability, and extensibility, in a user study. Our work shows a promising starting point for automated, model-independent description of changes in model-based engineering projects.

</details>


### [21] [Information is all you need: Requirements Engineering Quality Reframed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21319)
*Henning Femmer,Julian Frattini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种整体性需求工程质量理论，将需求工程视为领域知识信息粒子在角色与工件之间的传递过程，并通过仿真说明该理论可用于优化信息流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有需求工程质量研究往往分别关注需求工件质量或过程质量，且对“上下文”的讨论较为模糊。作者旨在以统一理论解释需求工程过程与产物的成功或失败。

Method: 理论以离散的领域知识单元“信息粒子”为基本对象，建模其从信息源（如利益相关者）经由角色和工件流向信息目标（如开发人员、测试人员）的过程；以信息传递的有效性与效率作为整体质量依据，并通过敏捷场景下的示例性仿真进行验证。

Result: 仿真表明，即使存在高质量需求规格说明，在敏捷环境中仍可能被绕过；原因可从信息流路径、传递成本和角色交互等过程属性中解释。该模型能够产生关于如何校准需求工程流程的可操作洞见。

Conclusion: 需求工程质量应被理解为信息流质量，而非仅是文档或单个活动的质量。该整体理论整合了工件导向与过程导向视角，并可作为分析和改进需求工程实践的统一框架。

Abstract: To move beyond this vague appeal to context, this vision proposes a novel holistic theory of requirements engineering (RE) quality. This theory models how information particles, i.e., discrete pieces of domain knowledge, are transferred between roles and artifacts. Since the RE process is ultimately an information transfer, holistic RE quality depends on the properties of information flow, i.e., how effectively and efficiently information is transferred from sources (like stakeholders) to targets (like developers and testers), uniting both artifact- and process-based perspectives on RE quality. In an exemplary simulation of the theory we illustrate why a high-quality specification gets bypassed in an agile context, thereby demonstrating that a simulation can provide actionable insights into calibrating the RE process to optimize the information flow. Beyond organizational applications, we envision that the theory can serve as a coherent theoretical framework for understanding the success or failure of RE processes and artifacts.

</details>


### [22] [Teaching Business Process Modeling to Leverage Soft Skills of Computing Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21344)
*Maria Istela Cagnin,Rick Kazman,Elisa Yumi Nakagawa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究表明，采用行业驱动的项目式教学开展业务流程建模，能够在提升计算机专业学生建模实践能力的同时，促进其职业素养、主动性、学习动机、沟通与团队协作等软技能的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着组织对软件系统的依赖加深，以及AI辅助开发提升技术生产率，计算机从业者仅具备技术能力已不足以形成就业竞争力。理解业务流程并能与利益相关者有效协作，成为构建符合组织需求的软件系统的重要能力，因此有必要评估业务流程建模教学对学生软技能培养的作用。

Method: 研究采用行业驱动的项目式学习方法，组织53名计算机专业学生与组织利益相关者进行实际互动，并完成业务流程建模任务。研究者系统收集学生反馈，重点识别其在项目实践中获得锻炼和提升的软技能类型。

Result: 学生反馈显示，业务流程建模项目不仅要求学生理解组织业务与需求，也促使其在真实协作情境中练习多项软技能。得到明显促进的能力包括职业素养、主动性、学习动机、沟通能力和团队合作能力。

Conclusion: 业务流程建模课程可作为计算机教育中兼顾技术能力与软技能培养的有效教学内容。通过引入真实行业情境、利益相关者互动和项目式实践，该类课程有助于学生适应AI辅助软件开发环境下对业务理解、协作沟通和职业能力提出的更高要求。

Abstract: Background and Context: Organizations have highly depended on software systems to realize their business processes and achieve strategic business goals and competitive advantage; hence, computing professionals who can understand these processes could more effectively build such systems. At the same time, while Artificial Intelligence (AI) has increased the productivity in developing these and other systems, professionals should be better prepared due to the competitiveness of the job market; thus, soft skills could be an important factor to ensure their position in this market. Objective: This paper aims to analyze and present the impact of teaching business process modeling to leverage key soft skills of future computing professionals. Method and Findings: Our method involved an industry-driven, project-based approach with 53 computing students who actively interacted with stakeholders and modeled the organization's business process. We systematically collected the students' feedback, including the soft skills practiced and demonstrating that professionalism, initiative, motivation, communication, and teamwork can be leveraged. Implications: The main implication of this work for the Computer Science Education research community is to provide evidence that teaching business process modeling subjects is particularly beneficial for future professionals, as it could contribute to preparing them regarding their soft skills for the era of AI-assisted software development.

</details>


### [23] [Toward Federated Cognitive Digital Twins over the Edge-to-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21357)
*Alessandra Somma,Alessio Bucaioni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出联邦认知数字孪生（FCDT）架构，将联邦式数字孪生的分布式协同能力与认知数字孪生的语义推理、可解释性和智能决策能力结合，用于提升复杂分布式信息物理系统中的可扩展性、实时响应性与决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字孪生架构多采用集中式、单体式设计，在大规模分布式环境中容易出现扩展性不足、通信时延高和韧性较弱等问题。联邦数字孪生虽然通过多个交互孪生体改善了系统扩展性，但智能能力仍主要集中在云端；认知数字孪生具备语义集成、高层推理和AI辅助决策能力，却难以自然融入分布式架构。因此，需要一种同时支持分布自治与认知智能的统一架构。

Method: 论文设计FCDT分层架构，将智能能力部署在边缘到云端的连续计算环境中。局部孪生体靠近物理资产，负责实时监测、局部数据处理及轻量级认知功能；全局孪生体负责跨域信息整合、系统级语义推理、仿真分析与协同协调。该架构通过多个孪生体之间的联邦交互实现分布式自治，并利用认知机制支持更高层次的理解、解释和决策。

Result: 摘要宣称，FCDT能够在复杂分布式信息物理系统中改善系统可扩展性、响应速度和决策能力。其核心预期收益包括：减少对单一云端智能中心的依赖，支持边缘侧快速响应；通过全局孪生体实现跨局部系统的协调优化；借助语义推理和AI能力增强决策的上下文理解与可解释性。摘要未提供具体实验设置、数据集、性能指标或量化对比结果。

Conclusion: 该研究的主要贡献是提出将“联邦化”与“认知化”统一的数字孪生架构思路，以兼顾分布式部署下的自治性和系统级智能。该方案在概念上适合对实时性、跨域协同和高层决策均有要求的复杂信息物理系统。后续需要通过原型实现和定量实验验证其通信开销、推理时延、联邦协调机制、故障恢复能力及相对于传统集中式和联邦数字孪生方案的实际增益。

Abstract: Digital Twins (DTs) are increasingly adopted to monitor, analyze, and optimize Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs) through continuous interaction between physical assets and their digital counterparts. However, current DT architectures often rely on centralized and monolithic designs, leading to scalability, latency, and resilience issues in distributed environment such as smart cities. Moreover, they provide limited support for semantic integration and high-level reasoning, reducing the effectiveness of DT-based decision-making. Recent studies on Federated Digital Twins (FDTs) have addressed scalability by decomposing complex systems into interacting twins, but they still largely centralize intelligence in cloud components. In parallel, Cognitive Digital Twins (CDTs) enhance DTs with semantic reasoning, explainability, and AI-driven decision support, yet they are typically difficult to integrate into distributed architectures. This paper proposes a Federated Cognitive Digital Twin (FCDT) architecture that combines federation and cognition within a unified approach. The architecture distributes intelligence across the edge-to-cloud continuum through local twins, which provide real-time monitoring and lightweight cognitive capabilities, and global twins, which perform system-level reasoning, simulation, and coordination. By integrating distributed autonomy with cognitive reasoning, the proposed approach improves scalability, responsiveness, and decision-making in complex distributed CPSs

</details>


### [24] [From Resource Flow to Executable Tests: Petri-Net-Guided LLM Test Generation for Concurrent Stateful Rust APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21530)
*Kaiwen Zhang,Guanjun Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向并发、有状态 Rust 库 API 的测试生成方法：以有色 Petri 网描述资源所有权、生命周期状态与因果依赖，生成合法深状态、近合法及偏序并发场景，并将其作为约束中间表示指导大语言模型合成可执行测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发状态型 API 的测试需要同时满足资源所有权、状态前置条件和线程交错约束。大语言模型虽能快速生成 Rust 测试代码，但常产生违反前置条件、覆盖深度不足或实际上顺序执行的伪并发测试；传统模型化与系统测试方法则语义控制强，但从抽象场景到可执行代码通常依赖大量人工实现。论文旨在弥合形式化场景设计与低成本测试代码具体化之间的差距。

Method: 方法以有色 Petri 网为核心建模形式：令牌表示 API 资源、生命周期条件及其相关属性，变迁表示 API 操作及状态转换；基于该模型导出三类测试场景，包括合法的深层状态场景、接近边界但可能触发错误处理路径的近合法场景，以及表达线程间因果关系的偏序并发场景。随后，场景被用作约束化中间表示输入大语言模型，以限制代码合成。论文进一步引入局部保真契约和结构修复循环，检查并修正生成代码是否保持原场景的资源、状态与依赖意图；通过 Petri 网引导的调度塑形，优先构造高冲突并发骨架并进行系统探索；最后使用分层语义预言机区分代码合成失败与目标 API 行为偏差。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验数据、对比基线、覆盖率提升、缺陷发现数量或运行开销。其宣称的产出是一个能够将形式化并发场景转化为可执行 Rust 测试的框架，并通过保真检查、修复和语义预言机提升生成测试的有效性与可诊断性。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献在于将 Petri 网场景模型作为大语言模型测试合成的受约束中间层，从而结合模型化测试的语义严谨性与大语言模型代码生成的低人工成本。该设计尤其面向资源所有权强约束、生命周期复杂且并发冲突显著的 Rust API；其实际价值仍需依赖后续实验验证生成测试的真实性、并发覆盖质量、修复成功率及相对于现有方法的成本收益。

Abstract: Concurrent stateful library APIs expose behavior through evolving resource ownership, lifecycle states, and competing interleavings. Large language models can synthesize executable Rust tests, but their outputs often violate API preconditions, remain shallow, or reduce concurrency to accidental sequential traces. Conversely, model-based and systematic testing techniques provide semantic control but commonly require substantial handwritten code to turn abstract scenarios into executable tests. This paper addresses the gap between formal scenario design and low-cost test concretization. We present a Petri-net-guided methodology for test generation over concurrent stateful Rust APIs. The method represents API resources, lifecycle conditions, and causal dependencies as colored tokens and transitions; derives legal deep-state, near-legal, and partial-order concurrent scenarios; and uses these scenarios as a constrained intermediate representation for LLM-based code synthesis. A local-faithfulness contract and structural repair loop preserve the modeled intent during concretization, while Petri-guided schedule shaping prioritizes high-conflict concurrency skeletons for systematic exploration. A layered semantic oracle then distinguishes synthesis failures from violations of the target API's expected behavior.

</details>

### [25] [Imprecise Probabilistic Programming, Precisely: Credal Sets via Graded Monads, BDDs, and Semiring-Parametric Inference (Functional Pearl)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20801)
*Jack Liell-Cock,Sam Staton*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Imp：一种嵌入 Haskell 的不精确概率编程 DSL。其核心观点是，不精确概率可通过将 BDD 变量的权重保留为自由参数来表示，因此无需改变现有的 BDD 编译与加权模型计数流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概率编程通常为随机变量指定唯一概率分布，但实际知识常包含认知不确定性，只能确定一组可能分布。直接采用凸幂集单子表示这类不确定性会破坏概率计算所需的交换性，影响独立随机计算的组合与推理。

Method: 作者将不精确硬币翻转编码为权重未固定的 BDD 变量，并利用加权模型计数对编译后的 BDD 进行推理。语言层面引入按有限“认知不确定性来源”集合索引的分级单子，以恢复计算的交换性；Haskell 的 GHC 类型系统在编译期检查相关约束。由于加权模型计数对半环参数化，同一 BDD 可在不同半环实例下执行计算。

Result: 该方法复用了离散概率语言中成熟的 BDD 编译和加权模型计数基础设施，不需要为不精确概率重新设计编译管线。单个已编译 BDD 可支持精确推理、可微推理以及区间界推理，而分级单子与类型系统能够静态保证不确定性来源组合的合法性。

Conclusion: 论文表明，不精确概率编程能够以较低实现代价整合进现有符号概率推理框架。Imp 通过自由权重、分级单子和参数化加权模型计数，同时获得表达认知不确定性、保持计算语义性质以及复用多种推理后端的能力。

Abstract: Imprecise probability generalizes standard probability theory by replacing a single distribution with a convex set of possible distributions. We show that this generalization requires no change to the standard BDD compilation and weighted model counting pipeline used by discrete probabilistic languages. An imprecise coin flip is simply a BDD variable whose weight is left free rather than fixed. We introduce Imp, a Haskell-embedded DSL for imprecise probabilistic programming. A graded monad, indexed by finite sets of named sources of epistemic uncertainty, restores the commutativity that the standard convex powerset monad lacks, and GHC's type system enforces this at compile time. Weighted model counting is parametric in the semiring, so the same compiled BDD supports exact, differentiable, and interval-bounded inference.

</details>

*Ehud Shapiro*

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Abstract: A grassroots platform is a multiagent distributed system in which multiple independent instances can form and operate independently of each other and of any global resource, yet may coalesce into ever larger instances, possibly resulting in a single global instance.  Grassroots platforms aim to offer an egalitarian/democratic alternative to  centralised/autocratic and decentralised/plutocratic global platforms.
  Here, we present Grassroots Logic Programs (GLP), a multiagent concurrent logic programming language designed for the implementation of grassroots platforms:  we recall the standard operational semantics of logic programs; introduce the concurrent operational semantics of   GLP as its restriction; recall multiagent atomic transactions; use them to introduce a multiagent operational semantics of GLP; and prove multiagent GLP to be grassroots. The grassroots social graph—the foundational grassroots platform on which all others are based—serves as a GLP programming example.

</details>


### [27] [chrKanren: Constraint Handling Rules in a Relational Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21204)
*Rafaello Sanna,William E. Byrd,Nada Amin*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出chrKanren：将约束处理规则（CHR）整合到纯关系式约束逻辑编程语言miniKanren中的方言，并在保证搜索与约束传播均保持完备性的前提下，扩展miniKanren的约束求解与程序综合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: miniKanren具备纯关系式编程和可逆计算的优势，但其原有约束机制在表达复杂、可定制的规则型约束求解器方面存在局限。CHR擅长通过声明式规则实现约束传播和专用求解器，因此需要解决CHR传播过程与miniKanren惰性搜索流之间的协调问题，以避免牺牲解空间探索的完备性。

Method: 作者设计chrKanren，将CHR规则及其约束存储、传播机制嵌入miniKanren的搜索流语义中。核心方法是协调约束传播与分支搜索的执行顺序和状态管理，使CHR能够在关系求值过程中增量地简化、推导或拒绝约束，同时维持miniKanren对可能解的完整枚举。论文还通过实例展示该框架对用户自定义数据结构的语义统一，以及结合类型与示例约束进行关系式解释器程序综合的实现方式。

Result: chrKanren表明CHR式约束传播可以与miniKanren搜索流有效集成，且两者的完备性得以保留。该语言能够支持比结构性统一更丰富的语义统一约束，并可用于类似MYTH风格的、由类型和输入输出示例共同引导的关系式解释器综合任务。

Conclusion: 该工作为miniKanren提供了可扩展的规则驱动约束求解框架，将CHR的约束传播能力与关系式搜索结合。其主要贡献在于给出保持完备性的集成方案，并证明该方案可支撑语义统一和类型—示例引导程序综合等高级应用。

Abstract: We present chrKanren, a dialect of the purely relational constraint logic programming language miniKanren which includes support for Constraint Handling Rules (CHR), a language for writing rule-based programs such as constraint solvers. We show how to integrate CHR's constraint propagation mechanism into the language of miniKanren search streams such that both processes remain complete. We also use chrKanren to illustrate novel applications of constraints in miniKanren, such as semantic unification of user-defined data structures and type-and-example-directed synthesis for relational interpreters in the style of MYTH.

</details>


### [28] [CaVE: A Constraint Storage Approach to Handling Integrity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21207)
*Xiangyu Guo,Ajay Bansal*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出CaVE（Constraints as Verifiers and Emitters）约束存储机制，使基于归结与扩展合一的stableKanren能够处理完整性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整性约束用于限制正常逻辑程序中目标的可接受结果，是非单调推理的重要组成部分。现有stableKanren缺乏可与其归结过程协同工作的完整性约束处理算法，因此需要新的约束存储与执行方案。

Method: 作者设计CaVE，将约束组织为可验证当前求解状态、并在必要时发射后续约束或条件的机制，从而与归结过程结合。论文还讨论了CaVE面向不同约束表达能力的变体，包括命题级、已实例化（grounded）及谓词级完整性约束；随后以Scheme在stableKanren中实现该方案。

Result: CaVE已被实现于stableKanren，并通过一系列带完整性约束的正常逻辑程序示例展示其使用方式。摘要表明，该方案可为stableKanren提供完整性约束支持，但未报告定量性能评测、复杂度分析或与其他系统的比较结果。

Conclusion: CaVE为stableKanren中的完整性约束处理提供了与归结兼容的实现路径，扩展了该系统表达和执行受约束正常逻辑程序的能力。其主要贡献是提出并实现了可覆盖多种约束层次的约束存储框架。

Abstract: This paper presents Constraints as Verifiers and Emitters (CaVE), a constraint storage approach for handling integrity constraints in stableKanren. stableKanren is a normal logic-program solver based on extended unification and resolution. Integrity constraints control the outcomes of goals in normal logic programs, which is critical for non-monotonic reasoning. There is no resolution-based algorithm for handling integrity constraints that can be used in stableKanren. Therefore, we design Constraints as Verifiers and Emitters (CaVE), a constraint storage that works with resolution to support integrity constraints. We discuss variants of CaVE with respect to the integrity constraints, ranging from propositional to grounded and predicate versions. We implement CaVE using Scheme in stableKanren and show a series of example normal programs written in stableKanren with integrity constraints.

</details>


### [29] [Top-down = Bottom-up: Sound and Complete Characterisations of Liveness by Multiparty Global Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21489)
*Kai Pischke,Nobuko Yoshida*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文证明：在采用精确子类型化的前提下，基于全局类型的自顶向下MPST方法与直接验证局部类型活性的自底向上方法具有完全相同的可类型化能力；同时，实验表明自顶向下方法效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为，自顶向下方法依赖全局类型及端点投影，因此可接受的通信程序集合可能严格小于自底向上方法。论文旨在反驳这一观点，并澄清两类MPST方法在活性验证与类型能力上的理论关系。

Method: 作者建立两种类型系统之间的双向等价：证明一个多方会话当且仅当能被自底向上系统判定为可类型且具有活性时，也能被自顶向下系统类型化。核心技术是主全局类型推断算法：该算法从任意一组满足活性的局部类型构造主全局类型，并结合精确子类型关系、端点投影、进程类型检查和局部类型推断完成理论证明与工具实现。

Result: 理论上，自顶向下与自底向上系统的可类型化集合完全一致，而非前者更弱。工程上，作者实现了覆盖两种策略的工具链，并在文献中的代表性案例上进行评估；结果显示，自顶向下策略的运行效率优于自底向上策略。

Conclusion: 全局类型及端点投影不会天然限制MPST系统的表达能力；只要使用精确的子类型化规则，自顶向下方法既能保持与自底向上方法相同的活性验证能力，又具有更好的实际分析效率。

Abstract: Multiparty session types (MPST) are a type discipline for concurrent and distributed systems, designed to ensure not only type safety and deadlock-freedom, but also liveness of typed communicating processes. Two main MPST methodologies, top-down and bottom-up, have been proposed and are integrated into a wide range of programming languages and tools. The top-down strategy starts by specifying the overall choreography of the protocol (called a global type), from which a set of local types that satisfy safety and liveness are generated by endpoint projection (EPP). Once each participant is type-checked against a generated local type, liveness of the set of typed processes is automatically ensured by construction. The bottom-up strategy directly checks whether local types inferred from processes satisfy liveness to enforce liveness of processes. Since the top-down strategy depends on global types and the EPP algorithms, it has often been considered that the top-down system offers strictly less typability than the bottom-up system. Our paper negates this belief. We prove that, using the precise subtyping for the subsumption rule, the top-down strategy offers exactly the same typability as the bottom-up system. More precisely, a multiparty session $M$ is typable and verified to be live by the bottom-up typing system if and only if $M$ is typable by the top-down typing system. The key to the proof is a principal global type inference algorithm which builds a principal global type from an arbitrary set of live local types. We have implemented the global type inference algorithm together with projection, process type checking and local type inference algorithms, and built a toolchain for both the top-down and bottom-up strategies. We evaluated our toolchain with representative examples from the literature, confirming that the top-down approach is more efficient than the bottom-up approach.

</details>

### [30] [AINTMA: Agentic AI Architecture for Autonomous Test Management with Generative Intelligence, Secure Cloud Communication and Adaptive Quality Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20452)
*Vinil Pasupuleti,Shyalendar Reddy Allala,Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu,Shrey Tyagi,Srinivasateja Songa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AINTMA提出一种面向云原生软件质量管理的多智能体架构，将测试发现、风险评估、强化学习排序、执行编排、生成式质量分析与安全监控整合为自治系统。摘要报告其在12个项目中的测试效率、缺陷控制和投资回报均有显著改善，但评价设计、数据分布及统计显著性尚未充分说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测试管理通常依赖人工规则、孤立工具和静态优先级策略，难以适应分布式云环境中的测试规模、变化速度和安全隔离需求。论文旨在通过多智能体协同、强化学习和大语言模型，将测试管理从被动执行转变为能够自主决策、持续学习并提供可解释质量洞察的质量智能体系。

Method: 系统包含六类专用智能体：测试发现、风险评估、强化学习优先级排序、执行编排、生成式质量智能和云安全监控。其核心排序模块将测试选择建模为马尔可夫决策过程，基于覆盖36个月历史执行记录的47项特征学习上下文策略；生成式模块利用大语言模型生成质量叙述、缺陷风险摘要和测试建议。基础设施采用云原生微服务，并以零信任API网关、OAuth2/JWT、加密消息传输和多租户隔离保障智能体间通信。实验覆盖12个异构项目、持续18个月，并与随机排序及商业基线进行比较。

Result: 摘要声称，AINTMA的测试优先级表现为88.4% APFD，高于随机策略的51.2%和最佳商业基线的82.1%；测试周期时间降低43%，缺陷逃逸率从8.3%下降至2.1%，投资回报率为340%，回收期为9个月。系统可扩展至5万以上测试用例，响应时间低于400毫秒；生成式质量模块获得4.3/5.0的开发者有用性评分。结果显示该方案在排序效果、测试效率、线上质量和可用性方面具有潜在优势。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是将多智能体自治协同、强化学习测试排序、生成式质量解释及云安全机制统一到测试管理架构中。摘要中的性能指标具有吸引力，但其可信度仍取决于完整论文是否说明项目类型与规模、训练和测试数据隔离、APFD计算细节、商业基线配置、消融实验、统计显著性、强化学习奖励设计，以及大语言模型输出的幻觉控制和安全评估。若这些证据充分，AINTMA可成为企业级云测试智能化的重要架构方案。

Abstract: Modern software quality assurance demands intelligent, autonomous systems capable of adaptive decision-making across distributed cloud environments. This paper presents AINTMA (Agentic Intelligent Test Management Architecture), a multi-agent agentic AI system that transforms traditional test management into an autonomous quality intelligence ecosystem. AINTMA deploys six specialized AI agents (Test Discovery, Risk Assessment, Reinforcement Learning Prioritization, Execution Orchestration, Generative Quality Intelligence, and Cloud Security Monitor) coordinated through a secure multi-agent communication framework over a cloud-native microservices infrastructure. The Generative Quality Intelligence agent employs large language models to produce plain language quality narratives, defect risk summaries, and data-augmented test recommendations. The RL Prioritization agent models test selection as a Markov Decision Process, learning contextual policies from large-scale historical test execution data (47 features, rolling 36-month window). Secure cloud communication is enforced through a zero-trust API gateway with OAuth2/JWT authentication, encrypted inter-agent messaging, and multi-tenant isolation. Evaluation across 12 heterogeneous software projects over 18 months demonstrates: 88.4% test prioritization accuracy (APFD, vs. 51.2% random, 82.1% best commercial baseline); 43% test cycle time reduction; defect escape rate reduced from 8.3% to 2.1%; 340% ROI at 9-month payback. The agentic architecture scales to 50,000+ test cases with sub-400ms response time, and the generative intelligence module achieves 4.3/5.0 developer usefulness rating. AINTMA demonstrates that agentic AI, combining autonomous multi-agent coordination, generative intelligence and secure smart connectivity, can fundamentally advance software quality management in cloud-scale enterprise environments.

</details>


### [31] [Marking the Wrong Symptoms: Evaluating LLM Watermarks in Medical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20462)
*Melanie Rieff,Robin Staab,Thibaud Gloaguen,Stefan Hegselmann,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了文本水印对医疗场景大语言模型与视觉语言模型性能的影响，发现通用基准指标可能掩盖水印导致的临床语义退化与推理错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型进入临床工作流，水印可用于追溯模型输出来源；但医疗文本对术语、否定表达和细粒度语义极其敏感，通用领域的水印评测无法充分反映其潜在风险。

Method: 研究评测了5种水印方案，覆盖11个大语言模型和7个视觉语言模型，并在单模态及多模态临床推理任务上进行比较。作者还构建了经人类医学专家验证的审计流程，用于系统检查医学推理质量、术语精确性、幻觉，以及图像发现的错误归因和遗漏。

Result: 水印会在多类医疗任务中造成显著性能下降，主要表现为词汇或术语损坏、虚构医学术语、临床推理质量下降，以及对医学图像所见的归因放大或关键信息遗漏。仅依赖汇总式通用指标时，这些具有临床后果的失败模式可能不易被发现。

Conclusion: 医疗领域部署带水印模型前，必须采用面向领域的细粒度评测，特别是结合专家审核的术语、推理和多模态证据一致性检查；否则，现有通用基准可能错误地将存在重要临床风险的水印模型评估为可接受。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical workflows, stressing the need for reliable traceability of model-generated output with watermarking. Yet, most watermarks are evaluated on general-purpose benchmarks, leaving domains like medicine, where small token-level perturbations can result in significant semantic changes, underexplored. In this work, we present the first rigorous study of how LLM watermarks affect medical performance, benchmarking 5 watermarking schemes across 11 LLMs and 7 VLMs on various tasks spanning unimodal and multimodal clinical reasoning. Importantly, we complement existing evaluations by introducing a human-expert-validated pipeline for systematically auditing medical reasoning quality, terminological precision, and induced hallucinations. Our results reveal that watermarking can induce substantial degradation across multiple failure modes, including lexical corruption, hallucinated terminology, and amplified misattribution or omission of image findings. Notably, we find that the absence of domain-specific analyses, combined with aggregate metrics that miss failures inherent to clinical text, can systematically obscure practical watermark-induced degradations. Our findings establish domain-specific evaluation as a prerequisite for the safe deployment of watermarked models in medicine, where current benchmarks can otherwise mask clinically consequential failures.

</details>


### [32] [ClickGuard: Detecting and Spoiling Clickbait News with Informativeness Measures and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20463)
*Wojciech Michaluk,Tymoteusz Urban,Mateusz Kubita,Soveatin Kuntur,Anna Wróblewska*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一款由人工智能驱动的浏览器扩展，用于在用户点击或阅读网页文章时识别标题党内容，并提供风险提示、可解释评分与文章摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标题党文章常通过夸张或误导性标题吸引点击，影响用户获取信息的效率与质量。现有方法多集中于离线检测，缺少面向真实浏览场景的即时预警、解释和内容概述功能。

Method: 系统比较了传统文本向量化方法、基于Transformer的文本嵌入及大语言模型嵌入，并将Transformer嵌入、语言学启发特征和自定义“baitness”评分融合。最终采用XGBoost分类模型进行标题党识别。浏览器扩展可在访问前后触发检测：访问后输出标题党概率，并依据系统分析指标解释预测结果；同时生成一至两句的全文摘要作为“标题党剧透”。

Result: 在开放的合并数据集上，所开发的XGBoost模型取得91%的F1分数。扩展能够为用户提供点击前或点击后的风险警告、百分比形式的标题党可能性评分、预测解释及文章简短摘要。

Conclusion: 该工作将混合式自然语言处理分类模型部署为浏览器端辅助工具，表明结合语义嵌入、人工设计语言特征和梯度提升模型可有效检测标题党。其实际价值在于将检测结果转化为可操作、可解释的浏览辅助信息，但摘要中未说明数据集规模、类别分布、跨领域泛化能力、实时推理延迟及与端到端深度模型的统计显著性比较。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven browser extension that identifies clickbait to help users avoid misleading Internet articles. Moving beyond traditional detection, the application employs a hybrid machine learning architecture that combines transformer-based embeddings with linguistically motivated features and a custom "baitness" score. After evaluating various natural language processing techniques -- from classic vectorizers to large language model (LLM) embeddings -- an XGBoost-based model was developed that achieves an F1-score of 91% on the open combined dataset. Most importantly, the tool can warn users before and after they access a clickbait article. After opening an article, the user receives a percentage score indicating the likelihood that it is clickbait. The prediction is explained based on the analyzed metrics, including those specifically developed within the proposed system. The browser extension also provides a clickbait spoiler -- a one- to two-sentence summary of the entire article. Demo video:https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4}{https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4

</details>


### [33] [Stochastic Sampling is Epistemically Shallow: The Dimensionality Gap Between Temperature Variation and Model Diversity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20464)
*Izhar Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文比较单模型多次随机采样与多模型集成在不确定性表征上的差异，结论是：自一致性能够提供可靠的逐题不确定性估计，但几乎无法揭示题目之间共享的、可解释的知识缺口结构；多样化模型集成则可检测到这种跨题结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型重复运行时的答案波动常被用于自一致性投票和置信度估计。作者关心的是，这些波动除反映单个问题的不确定性外，是否还能像多样化集成一样，揭示一组相关问题会共同出错的潜在结构，即模型知识缺失在题目维度上的低维模式。

Method: 作者在相同题目上比较两种设置：其一为单个模型在温度τ=1下重复运行100次；其二为24个不同语言模型在温度τ=0下各运行一次。研究覆盖五类任务族及MMLU、HellaSwag、GSM8K三个基准。通过构造回答结果的相关或协方差结构，并使用Marchenko--Pastur随机矩阵理论确定由有限采样噪声产生的特征值上界，检验超过该上界的显著低维信号。另以匹配题目难度的伯努利空模型进行500次蒙特卡洛比较。

Result: 在任一单模型的重复采样结果中，跨题变化结构最多只有一个特征维度显著高于随机噪声，说明多数答案波动可由独立采样误差解释。相反，在24模型集成中，有4个特征值超过随机矩阵噪声边界，表明不同模型的错误和不确定性存在多个显著的共享模式。匹配难度的伯努利空模型在500次蒙特卡洛实验中至多出现一次类似的显著结构，支持集成信号并非由题目难度差异或随机波动造成。

Conclusion: 自一致性适合估计每一道题的答案可靠性，并可借由多数投票提升预测表现；但它不能有效恢复不同题目之间的系统性知识结构。若目标是识别模型在哪些相关能力或知识维度上存在缺口，应使用具有差异性的模型集成，而非仅增加同一模型的随机重复采样次数。

Abstract: When a language model gives different answers on repeated runs, does that variation reveal what it does not know? Self-consistency turns the variation into a per-question uncertainty estimate via majority voting. But does the same variation reveal cross-question structure -- related questions flipping together, the way a diverse ensemble does? We compare two regimes on the same questions: one model run $100$ times at $τ=1$ versus an ensemble of $24$ LLMs run once each at $τ=0$. A Marchenko--Pastur random-matrix test separates signal from sampling noise on both sides. Within any single model, at most one dimension rises above noise across five families and three benchmarks (MMLU, HellaSwag, GSM8K). Across the ensemble, four eigenvalues clear the noise edge, while a matched-difficulty Bernoulli null produces at most one in $500$ Monte Carlo draws. Self-consistency gives accurate per-question uncertainty but no detectable cross-question structure; only a diverse ensemble surfaces what a model does not know.

</details>


### [34] [JAXBench: Benchmarking Autonomous TPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20466)
*Arya Tschand,Charles Hong,Julian Walker,Nina Cai,Shangkun Wang,Suvinay Subramanian,Sundar Dev,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Amir Yazdanbakhsh,Sethu Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JAXBench是面向Google Cloud TPU的JAX/Pallas内核优化基准套件，包含50个具有实际意义且存在优化空间的工作负载。实验表明，在Pallas这类文档相对稀缺的DSL上，提供TPU专属上下文比单纯增大模型规模更重要；结合检索上下文与搜索策略，可显著提升生成内核的正确性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核优化已有成熟的严格基准，可为自动化优化方法提供统一目标，但TPU领域缺少对应的公开评测体系。作者旨在构建一个TPU原生、贴近真实机器学习负载、可衡量代码生成系统正确性与性能的基准，以推动自动生成JAX/Pallas内核的研究和工程实践。

Method: JAXBench由50个JAX工作负载组成：17个来自公开MaxText库中的生产级机器学习算子，另外33个由KernelBench算子迁移而来，并重新设定问题规模以提高TPU v6e上MXU利用率。对其中8个生产算子，作者采用Tokamax公开库中的手工优化Pallas内核并调优块大小，作为专家性能上界。论文进一步评估四种基于反馈的Pallas候选内核生成方法，考察TPU文档上下文注入、模型生成正确率，以及搜索结构对最终性能的影响。

Result: 使用Gemini 3 Flash时，加入经过整理的TPU文档可将单次生成的正确率从5.8%提升至37.3%；该设置解决50个基准中的48个，并达到相对XLA的1.28倍几何平均加速。正确性建立后，搜索策略带来进一步收益：Autocomp的束搜索流程在全套基准上达到相对XLA的1.36倍几何平均加速。在8个具备手工优化参考实现的算子上，Autocomp达到1.60倍几何平均加速，恢复了Tokamax相对XLA 2.08倍上界中的大部分性能，但在专门优化的分页注意力和不规则长度注意力算子上仍有明显差距。

Conclusion: JAXBench填补了TPU自动内核优化基准的空白，并提供评测框架和基线结果。研究显示，TPU/Pallas内核生成的关键瓶颈首先是获得正确实现，而针对硬件与DSL的高质量上下文能有效缓解这一问题；在此基础上，结构化搜索是提升性能的重要机制。尽管自动方法已能取得可观加速，其在高度专用算子上的性能仍未达到专家手工优化水平，说明高质量TPU内核优化依然具有挑战性。

Abstract: Rigorous benchmarks have driven progress in autonomous GPU kernel performance optimization by establishing a shared target to hillclimb on, but no equivalent exists for TPUs. We present JAXBench, a TPU-native benchmark suite for AI-generated kernel optimization on Google Cloud TPUs. JAXBench comprises 50 JAX workloads that are both relevant and provide headroom for optimization. We extract 17 production ML operators from architectures in the public MaxText library such as Llama-3.1, DeepSeek-V3, Mixtral, Mamba-2, and AlphaFold2, and translate 33 operators from KernelBench that are validated for correctness and set with new problem sizes that achieve high TPU v6e MXU utilization. Eight of the 17 production operators ship with hand-optimized Pallas kernels from the public Tokamax library and block-size tuned to establish an expert upper-bound baseline. We evaluate four feedback-driven methods on generating candidate Pallas kernels for JAXBench. Across the full suite with Gemini 3 Flash, we find that target-specific context matters more than model scale on a sparsely-documented DSL like Pallas. Conditioning on curated TPU documentation raises per-sample correctness from 5.8% to 37.3% and solves 48 of 50 benchmarks at a 1.28x geomean speedup. Search structure yields significant gains once correctness is achieved, with Autocomp's beam-search pipeline reaching a 1.36x geomean speedup over XLA. On the 8 hand-tuned kernels, Autocomp reaches 1.60x geomean over XLA, recovering most of the 2.08x Tokamax upper bound but trailing on the specialized paged and ragged attention operators. High-quality TPU kernel optimization remains a challenging task, and we release the JAXBench benchmark, evaluation harness, and baseline results to support open source contributions.

</details>


### [35] [Representation Robustness Under Executable Reasoning Constraints in Large Language Models for Mathematical Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20520)
*Sagnik Nath,Edith Aurora Graf,Liang Zhang,Diego Zapata-Rivera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型在数学题不同等价表述下的鲁棒性，发现模型对故事题、文字方程、符号方程及同构改写高度敏感；代码增强能暴露部分潜在推理能力，但无法稳定消除表征脆弱性，且引入协议与执行层面的新失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有数学推理评测通常默认数学等价的题目表述可以互换，并将错误统一归因于推理能力不足。论文指出，这种做法忽略了输入表征和交互接口对模型表现的影响，因而需要区分真实的数学推理错误与由提示形式、输出协议或工具调用造成的接口失败。

Method: 作者构建包含数学上等价问题的整理数据集，系统生成故事题、文字方程、符号方程以及同构释义等变体。在直接生成答案条件下，对五个当代大语言模型进行评测，并统计不同等价表述之间的正确性翻转率。随后设置代码增强条件，要求模型将推理外化为可执行的Python代码，在本地运行和验证，并比较两种条件下的正确性、鲁棒性及失败类型。

Result: 直接作答时，模型在数学等价的不同表示之间经常改变对错，故事题、符号题和文字方程之间存在显著翻转率；即使仅进行保持数学结构不变的细微释义改写，也会出现系统性性能下降。代码执行可使部分直接提示下表现较弱的模型展现更强的潜在求解能力，但改善并不一致：失败会从难以观察的推理错误转移到输出格式不合规、工具交互协议错误或代码执行失败等环节。即便代码成功执行，表征敏感性通常仍然存在。
